`会议结束后，工作人员将签署的信件归档。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U00705 -->
The system archives inactive records to a separate storage tier.
`系统把不再活跃使用的记录转存到独立的存储层。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U00707 -->
An archivist arranged the papers by their original office of creation.
`一位档案工作人员按文件最初形成的部门整理了这些文件。`

<!-- WORD:W008 -->
# capacity

<!-- EXAMPLE:U00801 -->
Our capacity for giving care, love and attention is limited.
`我们给予照护、爱与关注的能力是有限的。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U00801 -->
Her mental capacity and temperament are as remarkable as his.
`她的智力和气质与他的一样出众。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U00801 -->
The course develops people's creative capacities.
`这门课程培养人们的创造能力。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U00802 -->
Bread factories are working at full capacity.
`面包工厂正在满负荷生产。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U00802 -->
The region is valued for its coal and vast electricity-generating capacity.
`这个地区因煤炭资源和巨大的发电能力而受到重视。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U00803 -->
The display describes an aircraft with a bomb-carrying capacity of 454 kg.
`展品说明介绍了一架载弹量为454千克的飞机。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U00804 -->
They inspected the fuel tanks, which had a capacity of 140 litres.
`他们检查了那些容量为140升的油箱。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U00804 -->
Grease 6 ramekin dishes of 150 ml (5-6 fl oz) capacity.
`给六个容量为150毫升（5至6液体盎司）的烤盅抹上油。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U00805 -->
Each stadium had a seating capacity of about 50,000.
`每座体育场约能容纳五万名观众就座。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U00806 -->
Toronto hospital maternity wards were filled to capacity.
`多伦多医院的产科病房已经住满。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U00807 -->
A capacity crowd of 76,000 people was at Wembley football stadium for the event.
`七万六千名观众挤满了温布利足球场，观看这项活动。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U00808 -->
She visited the Philippines in her capacity as a Special Representative of Unicef.
`她以联合国儿童基金会特别代表的身份访问了菲律宾。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U00808 -->
This article is written in a personal capacity.
`本文以个人身份撰写。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U00801 -->
People differ in their capacity for heat, pain, and other physical stresses.
`人们承受高温、疼痛及其他身体压力的能力各不相同。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U00807 -->
The hall held a capacity audience.
`大厅里座无虚席。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U00808 -->
He attended in a civil capacity.
`他以公民身份出席。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U00808 -->
I am speaking in my capacity as a critic.
`我现在是以评论员的身份发言。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U00806 -->
The workshop is working to capacity.
`这间工场正在满负荷运作。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U00809 -->
The court considered her capacity to enter into the contract.
`法院审查了她订立该合同的法律行为能力。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U00810 -->
A larger capacitance allows more charge to be stored at the same voltage.
`在相同电压下，更大的电容可以储存更多电荷。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U00811 -->
A capacitive sensor responds to a change in capacitance.
`电容式传感器会对电容的变化作出响应。`

<!-- WORD:W009 -->
# coherent

<!-- EXAMPLE:U00901 -->
He has failed to work out a coherent strategy for modernising the service.
`他未能为这项服务的现代化制定出一套连贯的策略。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U00901 -->
The President's policy is perfectly coherent.
`总统的政策完全前后一致。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U00907 -->
The campaign was widely criticised for making tactical mistakes and for a lack of coherence.
`这场行动因战术失误和缺乏整体连贯性而受到广泛批评。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U00902 -->
He's so calm when he answers questions in interviews. I wish I could be that coherent.
`他在访谈中回答问题时十分镇定。我希望自己也能表达得那么清楚。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U00907 -->
She lost consciousness and when she came round she still lacked coherence and focus.
`她曾失去意识，苏醒后说话仍不连贯，也难以集中注意力。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U00906 -->
The various elements of the novel fail to cohere.
`这部小说的各个要素未能形成连贯的整体。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U00906 -->
This coheres with Peel's championing of alternative music.
`这与皮尔对非主流音乐的支持是一致的。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U00905 -->
The empire could not cohere as a legitimate whole.
`这个帝国无法作为一个有合法基础的整体维系在一起。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U00907 -->
The anthology has a surprising sense of coherence.
`这部选集呈现出令人意外的整体连贯感。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U00911 -->
The man was almost incoherent with fear.
`这个男人吓得几乎语无伦次。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U00914 -->
Beth's incoherence told Amy that something was terribly wrong.
`贝丝说话语无伦次，让艾米意识到出了很严重的问题。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U00916 -->
He collapsed on the floor, mumbling incoherently.
`他倒在地上，含混不清地喃喃说着。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U00912 -->
The report identifies an incoherent set of objectives.
`报告指出了一组彼此不一致的目标。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U00914 -->
The debate exposed the general incoherence of government policy.
`这场辩论暴露出政府政策整体上缺乏一致性。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U00903 -->
The particles form a coherent layer rather than a loose powder.
`这些颗粒形成了一层黏结的材料，而不是松散粉末。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U00904 -->
The two coherent waves maintain a fixed phase relationship.
`这两列相干波保持固定的相位关系。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U00918 -->
The experiment measures the coherence of the light.
`这个实验测量光的相干性。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U00909 -->
She explained the sequence of events coherently.
`她有条理地解释了事情的先后经过。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U00910 -->
The beams combine coherently at the detector.
`这些光束在探测器处相干叠加。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U00913 -->
The sources emit mutually incoherent light.
`这些光源发出的光彼此非相干。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U00915 -->
The incoherence of the sources prevents a stable interference pattern.
`光源之间的非相干性使稳定干涉条纹无法形成。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U00917 -->
Light from independent thermal sources usually adds incoherently.
`来自独立热光源的光通常以非相干方式叠加。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U00908 -->
The binder improves the coherence of the pressed powder.
`黏结剂改善了压制粉末的结合性。`

<!-- WORD:W010 -->
# commodity

<!-- EXAMPLE:U01001 -->
The government increased prices on several basic commodities like bread and meat.
`政府提高了面包、肉类等几种基本商品的价格。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U01002 -->
For a parent working two jobs, free time is a precious commodity.
`对一位做两份工作的家长来说，空闲时间十分宝贵。`

<!-- WORD:W011 -->
# compromise

<!-- EXAMPLE:U01101 -->
Encourage your child to reach a compromise between what he wants and what you want.
`鼓励孩子在他的愿望和你的愿望之间达成折中。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U01101 -->
The government's policy of compromise is not universally popular.
`政府的妥协政策并非普遍受到欢迎。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U01102 -->
The government has compromised with its critics over monetary policies.
`政府在货币政策上已与批评者相互让步，达成妥协。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U01102 -->
'Nine,' said I. 'Nine thirty,' tried he. We compromised on nine fifteen.
`“九点。”我说。“九点半。”他试着提议。我们折中定在九点十五分。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U01102 -->
Israel had originally wanted $1 billion in aid, but compromised on the $650 million.
`以色列原本希望获得十亿美元援助，后来让步接受了六亿五千万美元。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U01104 -->
He compromised himself by accepting a bribe.
`他收受贿赂，损害了自己的名誉。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U01104 -->
He would rather shoot himself than compromise his principles.
`他宁可开枪自尽，也不愿损害自己的原则。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U01106 -->
How had this compromising picture come into the possession of the press?
`这张会使人名誉受损的照片是怎么落到媒体手里的？`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U01108 -->
She was a tough and uncompromising politician.
`她是一位强硬而不肯让步的政治人物。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U01110 -->
The company had once been uncompromisingly socialist.
`该公司曾坚定不移地奉行社会主义立场。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U01110 -->
He states uncompromisingly that he is opposed to any practices which oppress animals.
`他毫不退让地表明，自己反对任何压迫动物的做法。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U01109 -->
The review discusses a film of uncompromising brutality.
`这篇影评讨论了一部毫不掩饰残酷场面的电影。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U01110 -->
Visitors noticed the uncompromisingly modern decor.
`来访者注意到了那种彻底现代、毫不折中的装饰风格。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U01101 -->
They reached a compromise by bargaining.
`他们通过谈判达成了和解。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U01101 -->
The proposal is a compromise between ideals and material necessity.
`这一提案是在理想与物质需求之间作出的折中。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U01102 -->
They compromised over the terms.
`他们就条款达成了妥协。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U01103 -->
In the old legal wording, the parties compromised their dispute.
`用旧式法律措辞说，双方通过互让和解了这项争端。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U01105 -->
The chair acted as a compromiser, looking for terms both sides could accept.
`主席充当寻求折中的人，寻找双方都能接受的条件。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U01107 -->
The photograph showed them standing compromisingly close together.
`照片显示两人站得过分亲密，足以损害他们的名誉。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U01111 -->
Her uncompromisingness made agreement difficult.
`她不肯妥协的态度使达成协议变得困难。`

<!-- WORD:W012 -->
# conserve

<!-- EXAMPLE:U01201 -->
The republic's factories have closed for the weekend to conserve energy.
`共和国的工厂周末停工，以节约能源。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U01202 -->
The proposal calls for a big increase in U.S. aid to help developing countries conserve their forests.
`这项提案呼吁大幅增加美国援助，帮助发展中国家保护森林。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U01207 -->
The delegates attended a four-nation regional meeting on elephant conservation.
`代表们参加了一个四国大象保护地区会议。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U01207 -->
The programme supports tree-planting and other conservation projects.
`这个项目支持植树及其他环境保护活动。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U01208 -->
The second image was discovered during conservation of the painting.
`这幅画在保存修复过程中，被发现还藏有第二个图像。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U01209 -->
The fund supports projects aimed at promoting energy conservation.
`这只基金支持旨在促进节能的项目。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U01216 -->
The book examines the philosophy of modern Conservatism.
`这本书考察现代保守主义的政治哲学。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U01217 -->
The conservatism of the literary establishment in this country is astounding.
`这个国家文学界权威阶层的守旧程度令人惊讶。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U01218 -->
Most Conservative MPs appear happy with the government's reassurances.
`多数保守党议员似乎对政府的保证感到满意。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U01218 -->
The survey interviewed disenchanted Conservative voters.
`这项调查访问了感到失望的保守党选民。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U01219 -->
In 1951, the Conservatives were returned to power.
`1951年，保守党重新执政。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U01220 -->
The article describes counties whose citizens invariably support the most conservative candidate in any election.
`这篇文章描述了一些县，在那里，选民在任何选举中总是支持最保守的候选人。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U01221 -->
The new judge is 50-year-old David Souter who's regarded as a conservative.
`这位新任法官是五十岁的戴维·苏特，他被视为一位保守派人士。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U01222 -->
It is essentially a narrow and conservative approach to child care.
`这本质上是一种狭隘、守旧的育儿方式。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U01224 -->
The girl was well dressed, as usual, though in a more conservative style.
`那个女孩像往常一样衣着得体，不过风格更传统些。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U01227 -->
She was always very conservatively dressed when we went out.
`我们外出时，她总是穿得很传统。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U01225 -->
A conservative estimate of the bill, so far, is about £22,000.
`到目前为止，对这笔费用作保守估计，大约是两万两千英镑。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U01225 -->
This guess is probably on the conservative side.
`这个猜测可能偏保守。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U01228 -->
The bequest is conservatively estimated at £30 million.
`这笔遗赠保守估计有三千万英镑。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U01233 -->
The musician studied at the Paris Conservatoire.
`这位音乐家曾在巴黎音乐学院学习。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U01214 -->
Her father is now her court-appointed conservator.
`她的父亲现在是法院指定的法律监护人。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U01232 -->
The brochure describes the New England Conservatory of Music.
`这本手册介绍了新英格兰音乐学院。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U01224 -->
He was conservative in his dress.
`他穿着保守。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U01203 -->
The collision model conserves total momentum.
`这个碰撞模型保持总动量守恒。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U01204 -->
They conserve the fruit in sugar.
`他们用糖保存水果。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U01205 -->
A small dish of fruit conserve accompanied the meal.
`餐点配了一小碟糖渍水果。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U01206 -->
She spread strawberry conserve on the toast.
`她把高果量草莓酱抹在吐司上。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U01210 -->
Conservation of momentum constrains the result of an isolated collision.
`动量守恒约束孤立系统中碰撞的结果。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U01211 -->
The study discusses conservational measures for the wetland.
`这项研究讨论保护湿地的措施。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U01212 -->
A conservationist monitored the nesting site.
`一位自然保护工作者监测了这处筑巢地。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U01213 -->
The conservator cleaned the painting's surface.
`文物修复师清洁了画作表面。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U01215 -->
The conservator was responsible for the museum's collection.
`这位保管者负责博物馆的藏品。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U01223 -->
His proposals were conservative in tone, avoiding extremes.
`他的提议基调温和，避免极端。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U01226 -->
The model includes only conservative forces.
`这个模型只包含保守力。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U01229 -->
Her conservativeness made sudden changes unlikely.
`她谨慎保守的性格，使突然改变不大可能发生。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U01230 -->
Tender plants were kept in the conservatory over winter.
`不耐寒的植物冬季放在温室里。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U01231 -->
They read in the conservatory, enjoying the light through its large windows.
`他们在日光房里阅读，享受从大窗户透进的阳光。`

<!-- WORD:W013 -->
# constrain

<!-- EXAMPLE:U01301 -->
Many working parents are too often constrained by inflexible working hours.
`许多有工作的家长常常受到僵化工作时间的限制。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U01305 -->
For some reason he felt constrained to lower his voice.
`不知为什么，他觉得自己不得不压低声音。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U01309 -->
Their decision to abandon the trip was made because of financial constraints.
`他们因为经费限制而决定放弃这次旅行。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U01306 -->
He answered in a constrained voice.
`他用拘谨而不自然的声音回答。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U01306 -->
She greeted him in a constrained manner.
`她以拘谨的态度迎接他。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U01302 -->
The shortage constrained them to postpone the work.
`物资短缺迫使他们推迟了工作。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U01303 -->
The captives were constrained within the guarded enclosure.
`俘虏被关押在有守卫的围栏内。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U01304 -->
Threats constrained his assent rather than winning his agreement.
`威胁迫使他表示赞同，却没有真正说服他。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U01307 -->
She smiled constrainedly during the awkward introduction.
`在尴尬的介绍过程中，她勉强笑了笑。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U01308 -->
The prisoners lived under constant constraint.
`囚犯们一直处于自由受限的状态。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U01310 -->
There was a noticeable constraint in their conversation after the disagreement.
`发生分歧后，他们的谈话显得明显拘谨。`

<!-- WORD:W014 -->
# convention

<!-- EXAMPLE:U01401 -->
It's just a social convention that men don't wear skirts.
`男人不穿裙子，只是一种社会习俗。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U01401 -->
Despite her wish to defy convention, she had become pregnant and married at 21.
`尽管她想反抗传统，她还是在二十一岁时怀孕并结了婚。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U01402 -->
The essay examines the stylistic conventions of Egyptian art.
`这篇文章考察埃及艺术的风格惯例。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U01403 -->
The discussion concerns the U.N. convention on climate change.
`讨论涉及联合国气候变化公约。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U01403 -->
The text refers to the Geneva Convention.
`文本提到了《日内瓦公约》。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U01404 -->
She attended the annual convention of the Society of Professional Journalists.
`她参加了职业新闻工作者协会的年会。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U01405 -->
The report describes the Republican convention.
`这篇报道描述了共和党代表大会。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U01409 -->
The character is a respectable married woman with conventional opinions.
`这个角色是一位体面的已婚女性，观念循规蹈矩。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U01421 -->
People still wore their hair short and dressed conventionally.
`人们仍然留着短发，穿着传统。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U01410 -->
The report examines the risks and drawbacks of conventional family planning methods.
`这份报告考察常规计划生育方法的风险和缺点。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U01410 -->
This new memory stick holds twice as much information as a conventional pen drive.
`这款新存储棒存储的信息量是普通U盘的两倍。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U01421 -->
The farmer was fined for passing off conventionally-produced food as organic.
`这位农民因把常规生产的食品冒充有机食品而被罚款。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U01411 -->
We must reduce the danger of war by controlling nuclear, chemical and conventional arms.
`我们必须通过控制核武器、化学武器和常规武器来降低战争危险。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U01422 -->
Linus Pauling is an unconventional genius.
`莱纳斯·鲍林是一位不循常规的天才。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U01422 -->
He had rather unconventional work habits, preferring to work through the night.
`他的工作习惯很不寻常，喜欢通宵工作。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U01423 -->
The vaccine had been produced by an unconventional technique.
`这种疫苗此前采用了一种非常规技术生产。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U01423 -->
Despite his unconventional methods, he has inspired pupils more than anyone else.
`尽管方法不循常规，他对学生的激励却超过其他任何人。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U01416 -->
The essay questions the conventional wisdom that soccer is a minor sport in America.
`这篇文章质疑了足球在美国是一项小众运动的普遍看法。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U01406 -->
The Convention Parliament of 1689 met without a royal summons.
`1689年的国民公会议会未经王室召集便举行了会议。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U01407 -->
In bridge, a bidding convention can convey information about a hand to a partner.
`在桥牌中，约定叫可以向同伴传达一手牌的信息。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U01408 -->
The convention of the assembly required agreement on a meeting place.
`召集大会需要先就开会地点取得一致。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U01412 -->
The submarine uses conventional rather than nuclear power.
`这艘潜艇使用常规动力，而非核动力。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U01413 -->
The symbol has a conventional meaning shared by the users of the map.
`这个符号具有地图使用者共同约定的意义。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U01414 -->
His conventional expression of sympathy sounded insincere.
`他那套式的同情表达听起来缺少诚意。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U01415 -->
The figures are conventional, following an inherited artistic pattern rather than nature.
`这些人物形象采用因袭的画法，遵循传承的艺术程式，而非自然形态。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U01417 -->
The critic objected to the conventionalism of the design.
`评论者反对这一设计拘守惯例。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U01418 -->
A conventionalist may prefer established forms to experimentation.
`遵循惯例的人可能更喜欢既有形式，而不是实验。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U01419 -->
The conventionality of the ending disappointed her.
`结局落入俗套，令她失望。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U01420 -->
Repeated use can conventionalize a once unfamiliar symbol.
`反复使用可以使一个原本陌生的符号成为约定形式。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U01424 -->
The essay defends artistic unconventionalism.
`这篇文章为艺术上不拘惯例的态度辩护。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U01425 -->
The unconventionality of the arrangement attracted attention.
`这种安排的不同寻常之处引起了关注。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U01426 -->
The musicians arranged the instruments unconventionally.
`乐手们不按惯例布置乐器。`

<!-- WORD:W015 -->
# convey

<!-- EXAMPLE:U01501 -->
In every one of her pictures she conveys a sense of immediacy.
`她的每一幅画都传达出一种现场感。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U01501 -->
He also conveyed his views and the views of the bureaucracy.
`他也转达了自己的看法及官僚机构的看法。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U01502 -->
The railway company extended a branch line to Brightlingsea to convey fish direct to Billingsgate.
`铁路公司把一条支线延伸到布赖特灵西，以便将鱼直接运到比林斯盖特。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U01508 -->
Mahoney had never seen such a conveyance before.
`马奥尼以前从未见过这样的交通工具。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U01506 -->
The notice explains the conveyance of bicycles on Regional Railways trains.
`这则通知说明了区域铁路列车载运自行车的安排。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U01503 -->
The deed conveyed the property to the buyer.
`这份契据将房产所有权转给了买方。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U01504 -->
The duct conveys sound from one room to another.
`管道把声音从一个房间传到另一个房间。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U01505 -->
The rights are conveyable under the terms of the agreement.
`根据协议条款，这些权利可以转让。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U01507 -->
A diagram can help with the conveyance of a complex idea.
`示意图可以帮助传达复杂的想法。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U01509 -->
The solicitor completed the conveyance of the house.
`律师办妥了这所房屋的产权转让。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U01510 -->
Both parties signed the conveyance.
`双方都在产权转让契据上签了字。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U01511 -->
The conveyancer checked the title before completion.
`产权转让事务人员在交易完成前核查了产权。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U01512 -->
She specialises in residential conveyancing.
`她专门办理住宅产权转让业务。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U01513 -->
A conveyor carries the packed boxes to the loading area.
`一台传送装置把装好的箱子运到装货区。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U01514 -->
The material reduces the conveyance of sound through the wall.
`这种材料减少了声音通过墙体的传导。`

<!-- WORD:W016 -->
# cultivate

<!-- EXAMPLE:U01601 -->
She also cultivated a small garden of her own.
`她还耕种着自己的一小片园子。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U01609 -->
They crossed the few patches of cultivated land.
`他们穿过了那几小片耕地。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U01610 -->
The book covers the cultivation of fruits and vegetables.
`这本书介绍水果和蔬菜的栽培。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U01610 -->
Farmers with many acres under cultivation profited.
`有许多英亩耕地投入耕作的农民获得了收益。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U01605 -->
Cultivating a positive mental attitude towards yourself can reap tremendous benefits.
`培养积极看待自己的心态，能够带来很大益处。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U01611 -->
The discussion concerns the cultivation of a positive approach to life and health.
`讨论涉及如何培养积极对待生活与健康的态度。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U01606 -->
He may be monarch one day so we must cultivate him.
`他有朝一日可能成为君主，所以我们必须设法与他搞好关系。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U01606 -->
The President has carefully cultivated relationships with at least five influential ministers.
`总统一直悉心经营与至少五位有影响力的部长的关系。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U01608 -->
His mother was an elegant, cultivated woman.
`他的母亲是一位举止优雅、很有修养的女性。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U01609 -->
The collection includes a mixture of wild and cultivated varieties.
`这批收藏中既有野生品种，也有栽培品种。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U01602 -->
The farmer cultivated the soil between the rows to remove weeds.
`农民给行间的土壤松土，以清除杂草。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U01603 -->
They cultivate rice in the flooded fields.
`他们在灌水的田里种水稻。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U01604 -->
The laboratory cultivates the cells in a nutrient medium.
`实验室在营养培养基中培养这些细胞。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U01607 -->
Only a narrow strip of the hillside is cultivable.
`这片山坡只有一条狭窄地带可以耕作。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U01612 -->
Small cultivators sell part of their harvest at the local market.
`小农户把部分收成拿到当地市场出售。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U01613 -->
The cultivator loosens the top layer of soil.
`这台耕耘机翻松表层土壤。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U01610 -->
Cultivation of the bacteria requires a suitable nutrient medium.
`培养这些细菌需要适当的营养培养基。`

<!-- WORD:W017 -->
# diminish

<!-- EXAMPLE:U01701 -->
The threat of nuclear war has diminished.
`核战争的威胁已经减小。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U01701 -->
Federalism is intended to diminish the power of the central state.
`联邦制旨在削弱中央国家机构的权力。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U01701 -->
Universities are facing grave problems because of diminishing resources.
`资源不断减少，使大学面临严重问题。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U01701 -->
This could mean diminished public support for the war.
`这可能意味着公众对战争的支持减少。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U01702 -->
He never put her down or diminished her.
`他从不奚落或贬低她。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U01704 -->
They continued the project despite a slight diminution in asset value.
`尽管资产价值略有下降，他们仍继续推进项目。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U01703 -->
The intensity is diminishable by adjusting the control.
`调节控制器可以减弱其强度。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U01705 -->
The diminution amounted to one tenth of the original volume.
`减少量相当于原体积的十分之一。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U01706 -->
The second voice repeats the theme in diminution, using notes half as long.
`第二声部以减值方式重复主题，音符时值缩短为原来的一半。`

<!-- WORD:W018 -->
# discriminate

<!-- EXAMPLE:U01801 -->
He is incapable of discriminating between a good idea and a terrible one.
`他分不清一个好主意和一个糟糕主意。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U01802 -->
They believe the law discriminates against women.
`他们认为这项法律歧视女性。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U01802 -->
They proposed legislation which would discriminate in favour of racial minorities.
`他们提出了会给予少数族群优待的立法。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U01809 -->
More discriminating visitors now tend to shun the area.
`如今更有鉴赏力的游客往往会避开这个地区。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U01811 -->
She is exempt from sex discrimination laws.
`她不受性别歧视相关法律的约束。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U01811 -->
The inquiry examined discrimination against immigrants.
`调查审查了针对移民的歧视。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U01812 -->
They cooked without skill and ate without discrimination.
`他们做饭没有技巧，吃东西也毫无鉴赏力。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U01813 -->
The test measures colour discrimination.
`这项测试测量颜色辨别能力。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U01815 -->
The indiscriminate use of fertilisers is damaging to the environment.
`不加选择地使用肥料正在损害环境。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U01817 -->
The men opened fire indiscriminately.
`那些人不分目标地开火。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U01801 -->
He cannot discriminate between right and wrong.
`他不能辨别是非。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U01803 -->
Many things discriminate one person from another.
`许多特征使一个人与另一个人相区别。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U01804 -->
An experienced buyer learns to discriminate.
`有经验的买家会学会鉴别品质。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U01805 -->
The editor selected the photographs discriminately.
`编辑有选择地挑选了这些照片。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U01806 -->
The task tests the ear's discriminative ability.
`这项任务测试耳朵的辨别能力。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U01807 -->
The circuit includes a discriminator that separates signals by frequency.
`这个电路包含一个按频率区分信号的鉴别器。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U01808 -->
The rule was challenged as a discriminatory hiring practice.
`这项规定被质疑是一种带有歧视性的招聘做法。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U01810 -->
She chose the fabrics discriminatingly, noticing subtle differences in texture.
`她挑选布料时很有眼力，注意到了细微的质感差异。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U01814 -->
The argument depends on a discrimination between evidence and assumption.
`这个论证以区分证据与假设为基础。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U01816 -->
An indiscriminate heap of papers covered the desk.
`一堆杂乱的纸张铺满了桌子。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U01818 -->
The report criticised the indiscrimination of the selection process.
`报告批评了选拔过程不加区分的做法。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U01819 -->
An indiscriminative approach treats unlike cases as though they were identical.
`一种缺乏辨别的做法会把不同情况当成完全相同来处理。`

<!-- WORD:W019 -->
# diverse

<!-- EXAMPLE:U01901 -->
The street has shops selling a diverse range of gifts.
`这条街上有销售各式礼品的商店。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U01902 -->
Jones has a much more diverse and perhaps younger audience.
`琼斯的受众差异大得多，而且可能也更年轻。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U01907 -->
The company's troubles started only when it diversified into new products.
`这家公司的问题是在它扩展到新产品之后才出现的。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U01907 -->
Manufacturers have been encouraged to diversify.
`制造商受到鼓励，开展多种经营。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U01907 -->
These firms have been given a tough lesson in the need to diversify their markets.
`这些企业受到了一次严厉教训，认识到必须拓展多种市场。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U01909 -->
The seminar was to discuss diversification of agriculture.
`这次研讨会原计划讨论农业多样化。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U01904 -->
The exhibition explores the cultural diversity of British society.
`展览探讨英国社会的文化多样性。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U01905 -->
The council was set up in 2008 to promote diversity and inclusion in workplaces and communities.
`这个委员会于2008年成立，旨在推动工作场所和社区的多元参与与包容。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U01906 -->
His object is to gather as great a diversity of material as possible.
`他的目标是尽可能收集种类丰富的材料。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U01903 -->
The same theme is diversely expressed in the three works.
`同一主题在这三部作品中得到了不同表达。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U01908 -->
The fund diversified its investments across several industries.
`这只基金把投资分散到了几个行业。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U01910 -->
The old account records gifts from divers towns.
`这份旧记载录下了来自好几个城镇的礼物。`

<!-- WORD:W020 -->
# emerge

<!-- EXAMPLE:U02001 -->
Richard was waiting outside the door as she emerged.
`她走出来时，理查德正在门外等着。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U02001 -->
The postman emerged from his van soaked to the skin.
`邮递员从货车里出来时，全身已经湿透。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U02001 -->
The bark has holes made by the emerging adult beetle.
`树皮上有成虫钻出来时留下的孔洞。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U02003 -->
There is growing evidence that the economy is at last emerging from recession.
`越来越多的证据表明，经济终于正在走出衰退。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U02004 -->
The inquiry examined the growing corruption that has emerged in the past few years.
`调查审查了过去几年显露出来、日益严重的腐败问题。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U02004 -->
It soon emerged that neither the July nor August mortgage repayment had been collected.
`很快便发现，七月和八月的按揭还款都没有收取。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U02004 -->
The emerging caution over numbers is perhaps only to be expected.
`对数字逐渐显现的谨慎态度，或许本来就在预料之中。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U02005 -->
Vietnam has emerged as the world's third-biggest rice exporter.
`越南已崛起为世界第三大稻米出口国。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U02005 -->
New leaders have emerged.
`新的领导人物已经崭露头角。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U02006 -->
The chapter describes the new republic that emerged in October 1917.
`这一章描述了1917年十月出现的新共和国。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U02007 -->
New skills are demanded for emerging industries.
`新兴产业需要新的技能。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U02008 -->
The study examines the emergence of global players.
`这项研究考察了全球性重要参与者的出现。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U02010 -->
The archive documents an emergent state.
`档案记录了一个新成立国家的情况。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U02009 -->
The report describes an emergent nationalist movement.
`这份报告描述了一场新兴的民族主义运动。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U02005 -->
She emerged as a leading contender.
`她崭露头角，成为主要竞争者。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U02003 -->
They emerged unscathed.
`他们安然无恙地挺了过来。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U02002 -->
The seal emerged from the water beside the rocks.
`海豹在岩石旁从水里冒了出来。`

<!-- WORD:W021 -->
# enhance

<!-- EXAMPLE:U02101 -->
They'll be keen to enhance their reputation abroad.
`他们会很想提升自己在国外的声誉。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U02102 -->
He was concerned with the enhancement of the human condition.
`他关心的是如何改善人类的处境。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U02103 -->
Cinnamon is an excellent flavour enhancer.
`肉桂是一种出色的增味材料。`

<!-- WORD:W022 -->
# equity

<!-- EXAMPLE:U02203 -->
To capture his equity, Murphy must either sell or refinance.
`要把自己的房屋净权益变现，墨菲必须出售房屋或重新融资。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U02204 -->
The document describes a Personal Equity Plan.
`这份文件介绍了一项个人股票投资计划。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U02201 -->
We base this call on grounds of social justice and equity.
`我们基于社会正义与公平提出这一呼吁。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U02207 -->
We have an unsurpassed opportunity to transform the United States into a more prosperous, sustainable and equitable country.
`我们拥有一个无与伦比的机会，把美国变成一个更繁荣、可持续而且更公平的国家。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U02210 -->
The policy represents a real attempt to allocate scarce resources more equitably.
`这项政策体现了更公平分配稀缺资源的切实尝试。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U02211 -->
The welfare system is grossly inequitable and inefficient.
`这套福利制度极不公平，而且效率低下。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U02213 -->
Social imbalance worries him more than inequity of income.
`与收入不公相比，社会失衡更让他担忧。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U02202 -->
The judgment explains how equity supplements the strict legal rule.
`这份判决说明了衡平法如何补充严格的法律规则。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U02204 -->
The company financed the project by issuing new equity.
`公司通过发行新的股本权益为项目融资。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U02206 -->
Equity represents performers in the United Kingdom.
`Equity工会代表英国的演艺工作者。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U02208 -->
The trust gives the beneficiary an equitable interest in the property.
`这项信托给予受益人对该财产的衡平法权益。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U02209 -->
The panel questioned the equitableness of the distribution.
`审议小组质疑这种分配是否公平。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U02212 -->
The burden was inequitably shared between the two groups.
`负担在两个群体之间分配得不公平。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U02214 -->
If a home is worth £180,000 but the outstanding mortgage is £200,000, its owner has £20,000 of negative equity.
`如果一套房屋价值18万英镑，而未清按揭贷款为20万英镑，房主便处于两万英镑的负资产状态。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U02205 -->
The fund holds equities in several listed companies.
`这只基金持有几家上市公司的股票。`

<!-- WORD:W023 -->
# evolve

<!-- EXAMPLE:U02301 -->
The bright plumage of many male birds has evolved to attract females.
`许多雄鸟鲜艳的羽毛是在吸引雌鸟的过程中演化形成的。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U02301 -->
Maize evolved from a wild grass in Mexico.
`玉米由墨西哥的一种野生禾草演化而来。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U02301 -->
The account describes when amphibians evolved into reptiles.
`这段叙述描述了两栖类演化为爬行类的时期。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U02302 -->
The history follows a tiny airline which eventually evolved into Pakistan International Airlines.
`这段历史追溯了一家小航空公司最终发展为巴基斯坦国际航空公司的过程。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U02302 -->
Popular music evolved from folk songs.
`流行音乐由民歌逐渐发展而来。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U02302 -->
As medical knowledge evolves, beliefs change.
`随着医学知识发展，人们的认识也会改变。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U02303 -->
This was when he evolved the working method from which he has never departed.
`正是在那时，他形成了此后一直沿用的工作方法。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U02308 -->
The book discusses the evolution of plants and animals.
`这本书讨论植物和动物的进化。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U02308 -->
The course examines human evolution.
`这门课程研究人类进化。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U02307 -->
The chapter covers a crucial period in the evolution of modern physics.
`这一章讲述现代物理学发展中的一个关键时期。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U02315 -->
The diagram represents an evolutionary process.
`这幅图表示一个逐渐演化的过程。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U02315 -->
The fossils document a period of evolutionary change.
`这些化石记录了一段进化变化的时期。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U02309 -->
The reader follows the evolution of the plot.
`读者追随情节的渐次展开。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U02304 -->
The mechanism evolved the folded screen, spreading it out flat.
`机构将折叠的屏板展开，把它铺平。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U02305 -->
The reaction evolves a gas.
`这个反应会放出一种气体。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U02306 -->
The model treats the population as an evolvable system.
`这个模型把该种群视为能够演化的系统。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U02310 -->
The naval manual illustrates an evolution from line ahead to line abreast.
`这本海军手册图示了舰队从纵队改为横队的队形变换。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U02311 -->
The experiment records the evolution of heat during the reaction.
`这个实验记录反应过程中热量的释放。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U02312 -->
The mechanism controls the evolution of the folded structure.
`这一机构控制折叠结构向外展开的过程。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U02313 -->
The construction shows the evolution of the curve.
`这一几何作图展示了曲线的展开。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U02314 -->
The arithmetic text calls the extraction of a square root evolution.
`这本算术书把求平方根称为evolution。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U02316 -->
The feature is evolutionarily conserved across several related species.
`这一特征在几个相关物种中在进化上保持了下来。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U02317 -->
The lecture discusses evolutionism as an explanation of the origin of species.
`讲座讨论了以进化论解释物种起源的观点。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U02318 -->
An evolutionist accepts that species developed from earlier forms.
`进化论支持者接受物种由较早形态发展而来的解释。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U02319 -->
The essay places the argument within an evolutionistic framework.
`这篇文章把这一论证置于进化论的框架之中。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U02320 -->
The letters reveal the slow evolvement of her theory.
`这些信件呈现出她的理论缓慢形成的过程。`

<!-- WORD:W024 -->
# exploit

<!-- EXAMPLE:U02401 -->
Critics claim he exploited Black musicians for personal gain.
`批评者称，他利用黑人音乐家的劳动谋取个人利益。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U02401 -->
The report describes the plight of the exploited sugar cane workers.
`报告描述了受剥削甘蔗工人的困境。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U02408 -->
Extra payments should be made to protect the interests of the staff and prevent exploitation.
`应当额外支付报酬，以保护员工利益、防止剥削。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U02402 -->
The government and its opponents compete to exploit the troubles to their advantage.
`政府及其反对者竞相利用这些困境为自己谋取优势。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U02409 -->
The inquiry examined the exploitation of the famine by local politicians.
`调查审查了当地政治人物利用饥荒谋利的行为。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U02403 -->
Cary is hoping to exploit new opportunities in Europe.
`卡里希望充分利用欧洲的新机会。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U02404 -->
I think we're being very shortsighted in not exploiting our own coal.
`我认为，不开发我们自己的煤炭资源是很短视的做法。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U02410 -->
The report discusses the planned exploitation of its potential oil and natural gas reserves.
`这份报告讨论了对其潜在石油与天然气储量的拟议开发。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U02405 -->
His wartime exploits were later made into a film.
`他的战时事迹后来被拍成了电影。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U02406 -->
Exploitable raw materials were in short supply.
`可供开发利用的原材料很短缺。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U02406 -->
Of 27 new wells drilled, 16 have proved exploitable.
`在新钻的27口井中，有16口已证实具有开采价值。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U02407 -->
Your hope was I'd make some exploitable mistake.
`你原本盼着我犯下某个可供你利用的错误。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U02411 -->
The essay examines the exploitative nature of reality television.
`这篇文章探讨真人秀节目中剥削性的本质。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U02412 -->
The workers described the contractor as an exploiter.
`工人们把这名承包商称为剥削者。`

<!-- WORD:W025 -->
# friction

<!-- EXAMPLE:U02501 -->
Sara sensed that there had been friction between her children.
`萨拉察觉到，自己的几个孩子之间曾有过不和。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U02503 -->
The pistons are graphite-coated to reduce friction.
`活塞上涂了石墨，以减小摩擦阻力。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U02504 -->
A friction clutch transfers torque through pressed contact surfaces.
`摩擦离合器通过压紧的接触面传递转矩。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U02505 -->
The mechanic inspected the worn friction disc.
`机械师检查了磨损的摩擦片。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U02502 -->
Repeated friction polished the edge of the stone.
`反复摩擦把石头边缘磨得光滑了。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U02506 -->
Frictional heating raises the temperature of the contacting surfaces.
`摩擦发热会使接触面的温度升高。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U02507 -->
The calculation assumes a frictionless surface.
`这个计算假定表面没有摩擦。`

<!-- WORD:W026 -->
# habitat

<!-- EXAMPLE:U02601 -->
In its natural habitat, the hibiscus will grow up to 25ft.
`在自然生境中，木槿可以长到25英尺高。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U02602 -->
The compact shelter became their temporary habitat.
`这座小型庇护所成了他们临时的住处。`

<!-- WORD:W027 -->
# incentive

<!-- EXAMPLE:U02701 -->
There is little or no incentive to adopt such measures.
`几乎没有、甚至完全没有动力去采取这类措施。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U02702 -->
The agreement offers a financial incentive for higher output.
`协议为提高产量提供了金钱奖励。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U02703 -->
The firm introduced an incentive payment scheme.
`这家公司推出了一项激励报酬方案。`

<!-- WORD:W028 -->
# inherit

<!-- EXAMPLE:U02801 -->
He has no son to inherit his land.
`他没有儿子来继承自己的土地。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U02801 -->
She catalogued paintings that he inherited from his father.
`她为他从父亲那里继承的画作编了目录。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U02801 -->
The survey includes people with inherited wealth.
`这项调查包括通过继承拥有财富的人。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U02802 -->
The government inherited an impossible situation from its predecessors.
`政府从前任那里接手了一个极其棘手的局面。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U02803 -->
We inherit many of our physical characteristics from our parents.
`我们的许多身体特征遗传自父母。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U02803 -->
Her children have inherited her love of sport.
`她的孩子承袭了她对运动的热爱。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U02803 -->
Stammering is probably an inherited defect.
`口吃可能是一种遗传性缺陷。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U02804 -->
She feared losing her inheritance to her stepmother.
`她担心自己的遗产会落到继母手里。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U02805 -->
The account describes the situation that was Truman's inheritance as President.
`这段记载描述了杜鲁门就任总统时接手的局面。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U02806 -->
Eye colour shows your genetic inheritance.
`眼睛的颜色体现了你的遗传特征。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U02809 -->
They called themselves the proud inheritors of the Prussian military tradition.
`他们自称是普鲁士军事传统的自豪承接者。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U02808 -->
Two-thirds of inheritors promptly sold the houses they were left.
`三分之二的继承人很快就卖掉了留给他们的房屋。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U02801 -->
A younger son rarely inherits.
`幼子很少获得遗产。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U02807 -->
The records show the date of inheritance.
`这些记录显示了继承发生的日期。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U02810 -->
The deed names her as the inheritrix of the estate.
`契据把她列为这份地产的女性继承人。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U02811 -->
The agreement concerns inheritable rights.
`这份协议涉及可继承的权利。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U02812 -->
The old rule classified which heirs were inheritable.
`这项旧规则划定了哪些继承人具有继承资格。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U02813 -->
The case turned on the inheritability of the title.
`这起案件的关键在于该称号能否继承。`

<!-- WORD:W029 -->
# integrate

<!-- EXAMPLE:U02901 -->
He didn't integrate successfully into the Italian way of life.
`他没有成功融入意大利的生活方式。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U02901 -->
Integrating the kids with the community, finding them a role, is essential.
`让孩子们融入社区、为他们找到参与的角色，这很重要。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U02901 -->
The way Swedes integrate immigrants is, she feels, 100% more advanced.
`她觉得，瑞典人帮助移民融入社会的方式要先进一倍。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U02901 -->
If they want to integrate, that's fine with me.
`如果他们想融入进来，我没有意见。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U02909 -->
He thinks we are living in a fully integrated, supportive society.
`他认为，我们生活在一个完全融合、相互支持的社会中。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U02911 -->
The proposal supports the integration of people with disabilities into mainstream society.
`这项提案支持残障人士融入主流社会。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U02902 -->
Schools came to us because they wanted to integrate.
`学校来找我们，是因为它们想取消种族隔离。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U02902 -->
Encouraging teacher transfer would not, by itself, integrate the teaching corps.
`仅仅鼓励教师调动，并不能使教师队伍实现种族融合。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U02908 -->
The profile describes a Black honor student in Chicago's integrated Lincoln Park High School.
`这篇人物简介介绍了芝加哥实行种族融合教育的林肯公园高中的一位黑人优等生。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U02911 -->
Lots of people in Chicago see progress towards integration.
`芝加哥许多人看到了朝种族融合迈进的进展。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U02903 -->
The director discussed the problem of integrating the spoken passages with the musical numbers.
`导演讨论了如何把对白段落与音乐节目衔接起来的问题。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U02903 -->
Ann wanted the conservatory to integrate with the kitchen.
`安希望玻璃暖房与厨房融为一体。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U02903 -->
Little attempt was made to integrate the parts into a coherent whole.
`人们几乎没有尝试把这些部分整合成连贯的整体。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U02903 -->
Talks will now begin about integrating the activities of both companies.
`双方现在将开始讨论如何整合两家公司的业务活动。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U02909 -->
There is, he said, a lack of an integrated national transport policy.
`他说，目前缺少一套统一衔接的全国交通政策。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U02910 -->
The article discusses the closer integration of global capital markets.
`这篇文章讨论全球资本市场更紧密的整合。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U02908 -->
We believe that pupils of integrated schools will have more tolerant attitudes.
`我们相信，在种族或宗教融合学校就读的学生会更加包容。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U02904 -->
The missing sections were added to integrate the incomplete account.
`缺失的部分被补入，使这份不完整的叙述得到补全。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U02905 -->
Integrate the function over the interval from zero to one.
`对这个函数在零到一的区间上求积分。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U02906 -->
The instrument records the integrated value of the signal over one second.
`仪器记录该信号在一秒内的累计积分值。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U02907 -->
The argument treats the structure as an integrate whole.
`这个论证把该结构视为一个完整的整体。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U02912 -->
The chapter considers the integration of diverse elements of perception within a personality.
`这一章讨论不同知觉要素在一个人格中的整合。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U02913 -->
The integrationist opposed separate schools for different racial groups.
`这位主张融合的人反对不同种族分校就读。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U02914 -->
The module is integrable into the existing system.
`这个模块可以整合进现有系统。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U02915 -->
This continuous function is integrable on the closed interval.
`这个连续函数在该闭区间上可积。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U02916 -->
The proof establishes the function's integrability.
`这个证明确定了该函数的可积性。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U02917 -->
The course takes an integrative approach to history and art.
`这门课程以整合的方式处理历史与艺术。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U02918 -->
The integrator registers the accumulated signal.
`这台积分仪记录累计信号。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U02919 -->
The project needs an integrator who can connect the separate components.
`这个项目需要一个能把独立部件连接起来的整合者。`

<!-- WORD:W030 -->
# intervene

<!-- EXAMPLE:U03001 -->
The situation calmed down when police intervened.
`警方介入后，局势平静了下来。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U03001 -->
The Government is doing nothing to intervene in the crisis.
`政府没有采取任何行动介入这场危机。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U03002 -->
Hattie intervened and told me to stop it.
`哈蒂插话，叫我停下来。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U03002 -->
'I've told you he's not here,' Irena intervened.
`“我已经告诉你，他不在这里。”伊雷娜插话说。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U03003 -->
The South African mailboat arrived on Friday mornings unless bad weather intervened.
`只要没有恶劣天气打断航程，南非邮船就会在星期五早晨抵达。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U03010 -->
The book examines the role of the United States and its intervention in the internal affairs of many countries.
`这本书考察美国的角色及其干涉多国内政的行为。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U03010 -->
The debate concerned military interventions.
`辩论涉及军事干预。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U03011 -->
Her friends decided it was time for an intervention.
`她的朋友们决定，该共同劝她寻求帮助了。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U03013 -->
The article analyses the interventionist industrial policy of the government.
`这篇文章分析政府的干预型产业政策。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U03004 -->
Several years intervened between the discovery and publication.
`从发现到发表之间隔了好几年。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U03005 -->
A narrow valley intervenes between the two ridges.
`两道山脊之间隔着一条狭窄山谷。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U03006 -->
Chance may intervene even in a carefully planned experiment.
`即使在精心规划的实验中，偶然因素也可能介入。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U03007 -->
The organisation sought permission to intervene in the lawsuit.
`该组织请求获准以第三方身份参加诉讼。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U03008 -->
The intervenor submitted a separate argument to the court.
`参加诉讼的第三方向法院提交了一份独立论证。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U03009 -->
The map marks the intervenient strip of land.
`地图标出了介于其间的那条狭长土地。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U03012 -->
The mediator's intervention helped the parties resume talks.
`调停者的介入帮助各方重新开始会谈。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U03014 -->
An interventionist argued that the government should act.
`一位干预政策支持者认为，政府应当采取行动。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U03015 -->
The essay traces the history of economic interventionism.
`这篇文章追溯经济干预主义的历史。`

<!-- WORD:W031 -->
# isolate

<!-- EXAMPLE:U03101 -->
This policy could isolate the country from the other permanent members of the United Nations Security Council.
`这项政策可能使该国在联合国安理会其他常任理事国之间陷入孤立。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U03101 -->
Political influence is being used to shape public opinion and isolate critics.
`有人正在利用政治影响塑造舆论，并孤立批评者。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U03110 -->
They are finding themselves increasingly isolated within the teaching profession.
`他们发现自己在教师行业中越来越孤立。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U03112 -->
Diplomatic isolation could lead to economic disaster.
`外交孤立可能导致经济灾难。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U03102 -->
When he was thinking out a problem Tweed's habit was never to isolate himself in his room.
`思考问题时，特威德从不习惯把自己独自关在房间里。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U03102 -->
His radicalism and refusal to compromise isolated him.
`他的激进和拒绝妥协使他与别人疏离。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U03102 -->
Police officers had a siege mentality that isolated them from the people they served.
`警员抱有一种被围困的心态，使他们与自己服务的民众隔绝。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U03102 -->
But of course no one lives totally alone, isolated from the society around them.
`不过，当然没有人能完全独自生活，与周围社会隔绝。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U03105 -->
Suspected patients are isolated and kept under medical observation.
`疑似病人被隔离，并接受医学观察。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U03103 -->
Our anxieties can also be controlled by isolating thoughts, feelings and memories.
`把想法、感受和记忆分开辨识，也可以帮助控制焦虑。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U03103 -->
Gandhi said that those who isolate religion from politics don't understand the nature of either.
`甘地说，把宗教与政治割裂开来的人，对两者的本质都不了解。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U03104 -->
We can use genetic engineering techniques to isolate the gene that is responsible.
`我们可以用基因工程技术分离出起作用的那个基因。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U03104 -->
Researchers have isolated a new protein from the seeds of poppies.
`研究人员从罂粟种子中分离出了一种新蛋白质。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U03105 -->
You don't have to isolate them from the community.
`你不必把他们与社区隔离开来。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U03109 -->
Many of the refugee villages are in isolated areas.
`许多难民村位于偏远地区。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U03110 -->
Some patients may become very isolated and depressed.
`一些病人可能会变得很孤独、情绪低落。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U03111 -->
They said the allegations related to an isolated case of cheating.
`他们说，这些指控涉及一宗个别的作弊事件。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U03113 -->
Many deaf people have feelings of isolation and loneliness.
`许多失聪者有孤立和孤独的感受。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U03114 -->
Punishment cannot be discussed in isolation from social theory.
`不能脱离社会理论孤立地讨论惩罚。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U03115 -->
Malcolm works in isolation but I have no doubts about his abilities.
`马尔科姆独自工作，但我对他的能力毫不怀疑。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U03117 -->
The essay considers the perils of isolationism.
`这篇文章讨论孤立主义的危险。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U03118 -->
The government had to overcome isolationist opposition to the plan.
`政府必须克服孤立主义支持者对该计划的反对。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U03103 -->
They isolated the problem.
`他们把问题单独辨识了出来。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U03110 -->
She described feeling isolated.
`她描述了自己孤独无援的感受。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U03109 -->
They found an isolated farmhouse.
`他们找到了一所偏僻的农舍。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U03111 -->
This is an isolated example.
`这是一个个别的例外。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U03106 -->
The circuit is electrically isolated from the case.
`这个电路与外壳在电气上绝缘隔离。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U03107 -->
The compound is isolable from the mixture.
`这种化合物可以从混合物中分离取得。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U03108 -->
The isolator separates the instrument from vibration.
`隔离装置使仪器免受振动传递。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U03116 -->
The patient was transferred to an isolation ward.
`病人被转入了隔离病房。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U03102 -->
The partition isolates the work area from the rest of the room.
`隔断把工作区与房间其余部分隔开。`

<!-- WORD:W032 -->
# medium

<!-- EXAMPLE:U03201 -->
A medium dose produces severe nausea within hours.
`中等剂量会在几小时内引起严重恶心。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U03201 -->
He was of medium height with blond hair and light blue eyes.
`他中等身高，金发，浅蓝色眼睛。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U03202 -->
Foods that contain only medium levels of sodium are bread, cakes, milk, butter and margarine.
`仅含中等水平钠的食品有面包、蛋糕、牛奶、黄油和人造黄油。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U03202 -->
They served a sweetish, medium-strength beer.
`他们供应一种略甜、酒精强度中等的啤酒。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U03204 -->
Cook under a medium-hot grill.
`在中等热度的烤架下烹烤。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U03203 -->
Andrea has medium brown hair, grey eyes and very pale skin.
`安德烈娅的头发是中等深浅的棕色，眼睛灰色，皮肤十分白皙。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U03205 -->
In Sierra Leone, English is used as the medium of instruction for all primary education.
`在塞拉利昂，所有小学教育都用英语作为教学语言。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U03205 -->
But Artaud was increasingly dissatisfied with film as a medium.
`但阿尔托对电影这一表达媒介越来越不满意。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U03206 -->
Blood is the medium in which oxygen is carried to all parts of the body.
`血液是把氧输送到全身各处的介质。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U03206 -->
Hyatt has found a way of creating these qualities using the more permanent medium of oil paint.
`海厄特找到了利用更持久的油画颜料材料来创造这些特性的方法。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U03215 -->
I aim to strike a happy medium between playing entertaining football and getting results.
`我的目标是在踢出好看的足球和取得比赛成绩之间找到合理的平衡。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U03219 -->
The article examines mass media coverage of the issue.
`这篇文章考察大众媒体对这一问题的报道。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U03218 -->
It is hard work and not a glamorous job as portrayed by the media.
`这份工作很辛苦，并不像媒体描绘的那样光鲜。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U03218 -->
The study examines bias in the news media.
`这项研究考察新闻媒体中的偏见。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U03218 -->
Media coverage of cycling in July was pretty impressive.
`七月份媒体对自行车运动的报道相当出色。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U03221 -->
The brochure describes multimedia theme-park attractions and virtual-reality displays.
`这本手册介绍多媒体主题乐园游乐项目和虚拟现实展示。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U03215 -->
They tried to find a happy medium.
`他们试图找到合理的折中。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U03205 -->
The message reaches listeners through the medium of sound.
`信息通过声音媒介传到听众那里。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U03205 -->
The debate reached viewers through the medium of television.
`辩论通过电视媒介传到观众那里。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U03207 -->
The diagram shows light passing from one medium into another.
`图中显示光从一种介质进入另一种介质。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U03208 -->
The shape of a fish is ideal for its fluid medium.
`鱼的外形非常适合它所处的流体环境。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U03209 -->
The photograph shows researchers growing mould on the surface of a medium.
`照片显示研究人员在培养基表面培养霉菌。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U03210 -->
The report describes the medium through which money is raised.
`报告描述了筹集资金的途径。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U03211 -->
The essay explores language as an artistic medium.
`这篇文章探讨作为艺术表现形式的语言。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U03201 -->
She is of medium height.
`她中等身高。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U03221 -->
The course introduces multimedia applications.
`这门课程介绍多媒体应用。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U03212 -->
The painter mixed the pigment with an oil-based medium.
`画家把色料与油性调和剂混合。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U03213 -->
The medium claimed to carry messages from the dead.
`那位通灵人声称能传递死者的信息。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U03214 -->
They sought a medium between the two extremes.
`他们寻求两个极端之间的中间状态。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U03216 -->
The historian studies mediumism and the public role of mediumship.
`这位历史学者研究通灵活动及通灵身份的公共角色。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U03217 -->
The archive contains accounts of mediumistic performances.
`档案保存着对通灵表演的记述。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U03220 -->
The school staged a multimedia performance combining dance, projected images and music.
`学校上演了一场结合舞蹈、投影图像与音乐的多媒体演出。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U03222 -->
Multimedia in the lesson combined books with television recordings.
`这节课的多媒体教学把书本和电视录像结合起来。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U03221 -->
Hypermedia links a passage of text to audio and video.
`超媒体把一段文字与音频和视频链接起来。`

<!-- WORD:W033 -->
# migrate

<!-- EXAMPLE:U03301 -->
People migrate to cities like Jakarta in search of work.
`人们迁往雅加达这样的城市寻找工作。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U03301 -->
Farmers have learned that they have to migrate if they want to survive.
`农民已经明白，要生存下去就得迁移。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U03304 -->
The study examines the migration of Soviet Jews to Israel.
`这项研究考察苏联犹太人迁往以色列的过程。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U03302 -->
Most birds have to fly long distances to migrate.
`大多数鸟类为了迁徙，必须飞行很长距离。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U03302 -->
The article describes a dam system that kills the fish as they migrate from streams to the ocean.
`这篇文章描述了一个在鱼从溪流迁往海洋时会导致其死亡的水坝系统。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U03304 -->
The film follows the migration of animals in the Serengeti.
`这部影片追踪塞伦盖蒂动物的迁徙。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U03309 -->
The government divides asylum-seekers into economic migrants and genuine refugees.
`政府把寻求庇护者分为经济移民和真正的难民。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U03309 -->
The report describes migrant workers following harvests northward.
`这份报告描述了随收获季节向北流动的务工者。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U03310 -->
Migrant birds shelter in the reeds.
`迁徙的鸟类在芦苇丛中栖息。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U03308 -->
The records document migratory farm labour.
`这些记录呈现了流动性农业劳动力的情况。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U03303 -->
The dune migrates inland under the prevailing wind.
`沙丘在盛行风作用下向内陆移动。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U03305 -->
The map shows a migrational pattern along the coast.
`地图显示出沿海岸的一种迁移模式。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U03306 -->
The salmon is a long-distance migrator.
`鲑鱼是一种长距离迁徙动物。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U03307 -->
The wetland provides a resting place for migratory birds.
`这片湿地为候鸟提供歇脚处。`

<!-- WORD:W034 -->
# modify

<!-- EXAMPLE:U03401 -->
The club members did agree to modify their recruitment policy.
`俱乐部成员确实同意修改招募政策。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U03401 -->
The plane was a modified version of the C-130.
`这架飞机是C-130的一个改装版本。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U03409 -->
Relatively minor modifications were required.
`需要作出的改动相对较小。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U03403 -->
It is a rule of English that adjectives generally precede the noun they modify: we say 'a good cry', not 'a cry good'.
`英语有这样一条规则：形容词通常位于它所修饰的名词之前；我们说a good cry，而不是a cry good。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U03402 -->
They agreed to modify their demands.
`他们同意降低要求。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U03405 -->
The recipe uses modified starch.
`这个配方使用改性淀粉。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U03404 -->
The sound change modified the vowel in that syllable.
`这一音变改变了该音节中的元音。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U03406 -->
The settings remain modifiable after installation.
`安装后，这些设置仍可修改。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U03407 -->
The clause has a modificatory effect on the earlier provision.
`这一条款对先前规定起修改作用。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U03408 -->
Modification of the design took several weeks.
`修改这项设计花了几个星期。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U03410 -->
The additive acts as a viscosity modifier.
`这种添加剂起黏度调节剂的作用。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U03411 -->
In a good family house, both good and family function as modifiers of house.
`在a good family house中，good和family都充当house的修饰语。`

<!-- WORD:W035 -->
# momentum

<!-- EXAMPLE:U03501 -->
This campaign is really gaining momentum.
`这场行动确实越来越有势头。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U03502 -->
At the same velocity, doubling the mass doubles the momentum.
`速度矢量相同时，质量加倍会使动量加倍。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U03503 -->
The bicycle's momentum carried it a little farther up the slope.
`自行车已有的冲势使它又向坡上滑行了一小段。`

<!-- WORD:W036 -->
# mutual

<!-- EXAMPLE:U03601 -->
The East and the West can work together for their mutual benefit and progress.
`东方与西方可以合作，实现相互的利益与进步。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U03601 -->
It's plain that he adores his daughter, and the feeling is mutual.
`显然，他很疼爱女儿，而女儿也同样爱他。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U03606 -->
Attempts to reach a mutually agreed solution had been fruitless.
`试图达成各方共同同意的解决方案，结果毫无收获。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U03602 -->
They do, however, share a mutual interest in design.
`不过，他们确实共同对设计感兴趣。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U03602 -->
We were introduced by a mutual friend.
`一位共同的朋友介绍我们认识。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U03604 -->
Britain's third-largest building society abandoned its mutual status and became a bank.
`英国第三大建房互助协会放弃了成员互助的组织性质，转为一家银行。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U03607 -->
They both have learnt that ambition and successful fatherhood can be mutually exclusive.
`他们都已体会到，事业雄心和成功履行父亲职责有时可能无法兼得。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U03601 -->
Their letters show mutual affection.
`他们的信件流露出相互的爱慕。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U03602 -->
She is a mutual friend.
`她是我们共同的朋友。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U03602 -->
They have a mutual interest.
`他们有共同利益。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U03603 -->
They remained mutual well-wishers after the disagreement.
`发生分歧后，他们仍彼此祝好。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U03603 -->
The two organisations are mutual beneficiaries of the arrangement.
`这项安排使两个组织彼此受益。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U03605 -->
The partnership depends on mutuality rather than one-sided assistance.
`这种伙伴关系依赖相互性，而非单方面援助。`

<!-- WORD:W037 -->
# orbit

<!-- EXAMPLE:U03701 -->
Mars and Earth have orbits which change with time.
`火星和地球的轨道会随时间改变。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U03702 -->
The planet is probably in orbit around a small star.
`这颗行星很可能正围绕一颗小恒星运行。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U03708 -->
In 1957 the Soviet Union launched the first satellite to orbit the Earth.
`1957年，苏联发射了第一颗绕地球运行的人造卫星。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U03705 -->
He is a man who still commands enormous respect within the orbit of football club management.
`在足球俱乐部管理界，他仍然深受尊敬。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U03713 -->
An early idea for a London orbital road, a predecessor of the M25, was proposed in 1905.
`1905年曾有人提出伦敦环城道路的早期设想，这是后来M25的前身。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U03711 -->
The newly discovered world followed an orbital path unlike that of any other planet.
`这颗新发现的星球沿着一条与其他任何行星都不同的轨道运行。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U03703 -->
The spacecraft completed three orbits before the manoeuvre.
`航天器在机动操作前完成了三圈运行。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U03704 -->
The Bohr model represents the electron in an orbit around the nucleus.
`玻尔模型把电子表示为沿原子核周围的一条轨道运动。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U03706 -->
The skull preserves the left orbit.
`这件头骨保存着左侧眼窝。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U03707 -->
The bird has a conspicuous ring around its orbit.
`这只鸟的眼周有一圈醒目的环纹。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U03709 -->
The aircraft orbited above the field while waiting to land.
`飞机等待降落时，在机场上方盘旋。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U03710 -->
The launcher can orbit a small satellite.
`这枚运载火箭能够把一颗小卫星送入轨道。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U03712 -->
The scan shows the orbital bones.
`扫描图像显示了眼眶骨。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U03714 -->
An orbital describes a possible electron-density pattern around an atomic nucleus.
`一个轨函数描述原子核周围一种可能的电子密度模式。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U03715 -->
The orbiter carries instruments for observing the planet below.
`这艘轨道飞行器携带仪器，观测下方的行星。`

<!-- WORD:W038 -->
# persist

<!-- EXAMPLE:U03801 -->
Contact your doctor if the cough persists.
`如果咳嗽持续，请联系医生。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U03801 -->
These problems persisted for much of the decade.
`这些问题在这十年的大部分时间里一直存在。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U03802 -->
Why does Britain persist in running down its defence forces?
`英国为什么执意继续削减国防力量？`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U03802 -->
He urged the United States to persist with its efforts to bring about peace.
`他敦促美国继续坚持促成和平的努力。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U03802 -->
'You haven't answered me,' she persisted.
`“你还没有回答我。”她坚持追问。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U03802 -->
When I set my mind to something, I persist.
`我一旦下定决心做某件事，就会坚持下去。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U03803 -->
Skill comes only with practice, patience and persistence.
`技能只能通过练习、耐心和坚持获得。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U03804 -->
The statement expressed concern at the persistence of inflation and high interest rates.
`这份声明对通货膨胀和高利率持续存在表示担忧。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U03805 -->
His cough grew more persistent until it never stopped.
`他的咳嗽越来越频繁，最后一直咳个不停。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U03805 -->
Shoppers picked their way through puddles caused by persistent rain.
`购物者小心穿过持续降雨造成的水洼。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U03806 -->
She is a persistent critic of the government's transport policies.
`她一直坚持批评政府的交通政策。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U03809 -->
The allegations have been persistently denied by ministers.
`部长们一再否认这些指控。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U03810 -->
Rachel gently but persistently imposed her will upon Douglas.
`雷切尔温和却坚持不懈地让道格拉斯接受她的意愿。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U03807 -->
He complained about the persistent nagging.
`他抱怨那些没完没了的唠叨。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U03808 -->
A persistent calyx remains around the fruit after flowering.
`宿存花萼在开花后仍留在果实周围。`

<!-- WORD:W039 -->
# pigment

<!-- EXAMPLE:U03901 -->
The Romans used natural pigments on their fabrics and walls.
`罗马人在织物和墙面上使用天然颜料。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U03905 -->
I have a skin disorder, it destroys the pigmentation in my skin.
`我有一种皮肤疾病，它破坏了我皮肤的天然色素呈色。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U03902 -->
Chlorophyll is a pigment found in plants.
`叶绿素是植物中存在的一种色素。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U03903 -->
The maker pigments the resin before casting it.
`制作者在浇铸前给树脂着色。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U03904 -->
The study examines pigmentary changes in the tissue.
`这项研究考察组织中的色素相关变化。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U03906 -->
The examination noted excessive pigmentation in the affected tissue.
`检查记录了受影响组织中过度的色素沉积。`

<!-- WORD:W040 -->
# precise

<!-- EXAMPLE:U04001 -->
At that precise moment I felt sorry for him and didn't want to hurt him.
`就在那个时刻，我为他感到难过，不愿伤害他。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U04001 -->
The precise location of the wreck was discovered in 1988.
`沉船的确切位置于1988年被发现。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U04001 -->
He was not clear on the precise nature of his mission.
`他不清楚自己任务的确切性质。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U04002 -->
He does not talk too much and what he has to say is precise and to the point.
`他话不多，所说的内容准确而切题。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U04005 -->
More than a week ago, Thursday evening to be precise, she was at her evening class.
`一个多星期前，确切说是星期四晚上，她当时在上夜校。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U04013 -->
The charges were vague and imprecise.
`这些指控含糊不清，不够准确。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U04015 -->
This served to hide the confusion and imprecision in their thinking.
`这掩盖了他们思路中的混乱与不精确。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U04007 -->
Nobody knows precisely how many people are still living in the camp.
`没有人确切知道营地里还住着多少人。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U04007 -->
The meeting began at precisely 4.00 p.m.
`会议在下午四点整开始。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U04008 -->
Children come to zoos precisely to see captive animals.
`孩子们来动物园，恰恰就是为了看圈养的动物。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U04009 -->
'Did you find yourself wondering what went wrong?'—'Precisely.'
`“你是不是不由得想，到底出了什么问题？”——“正是。”`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U04010 -->
The interior is planned with a precision the military would be proud of.
`内部规划之精细，连军方都会引以为豪。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U04002 -->
She gave a precise description.
`她作了准确的描述。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U04005 -->
There were twelve visitors, to be precise.
`确切地说，当时有十二位来访者。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U04003 -->
They gave us a precise quote for installation.
`他们给了我们一份准确的安装报价。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U04001 -->
The bell rang at that precise moment.
`钟声恰恰在那个时刻响起。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U04012 -->
The laboratory uses precision instruments.
`实验室使用精密仪器。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U04012 -->
The experiment requires precision timing.
`这个实验需要精确计时。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U04004 -->
He was precise in observing every procedural rule.
`他对每一条程序规则都遵守得一丝不苟。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U04006 -->
The editor valued the preciseness of the wording.
`编辑重视措辞的精确性。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U04011 -->
The report states the precision of each measurement.
`这份报告注明每项测量的精密程度。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U04014 -->
The boundary was imprecisely drawn.
`这条边界画得不准确。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U04016 -->
Precisionism reduced industrial scenes to controlled geometric forms.
`精确主义把工业景象简化为控制严整的几何形式。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U04017 -->
As a precisionist, she insisted that every term be defined.
`作为一个严格求准的人，她坚持要求每个术语都得到定义。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U04018 -->
Charles Sheeler is associated with the Precisionists.
`查尔斯·希勒与精确主义艺术家群体相关联。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U04019 -->
The painting has a Precisionist treatment of industrial architecture.
`这幅画以精确主义方式处理工业建筑。`

<!-- WORD:W041 -->
# restore

<!-- EXAMPLE:U04101 -->
The army has recently been brought in to restore order.
`军队最近被调来恢复秩序。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U04111 -->
His visit is expected to lead to the restoration of diplomatic relations.
`预计他的访问将促成外交关系恢复。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U04102 -->
We will restore her to health but it may take time.
`我们会帮助她恢复健康，但这可能需要时间。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U04102 -->
His country desperately needs Western aid to restore its ailing economy.
`他的国家急需西方援助，以恢复陷入困境的经济。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U04112 -->
I owe the restoration of my hearing to this remarkable new technique.
`我的听力能够恢复，要归功于这项了不起的新技术。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U04103 -->
The project employs experts who specialise in examining and restoring ancient parchments.
`这个项目聘用了专门检查和修复古代羊皮纸的专家。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U04113 -->
I specialized in the restoration of old houses.
`我专门从事老房屋修复。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U04104 -->
The following day their horses and goods were restored to them.
`第二天，他们的马匹和货物被归还给了他们。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U04117 -->
The programme includes a Restoration comedy.
`演出节目包括一部英国王政复辟时期的喜剧。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U04118 -->
She opened the door to her bedroom, thinking how restorative a hot bath would feel tonight.
`她打开卧室门，想着今晚洗个热水澡该有多解乏。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U04119 -->
Seven days off could be a wonderful restorative.
`休息七天可能是恢复精神的好办法。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U04110 -->
She works as an antiques restorer.
`她从事古董修复工作。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U04119 -->
He needs a restorative.
`他需要一些能恢复元气的东西。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U04105 -->
The ruling restored her to office.
`这项裁决使她恢复了职位。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U04106 -->
After reading, he restored the book to its place.
`读完后，他把书放回原处。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U04107 -->
The drawing restores the building to its supposed original form.
`这幅图按推测复原了建筑的原始形态。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U04108 -->
The editor restored the missing word in brackets.
`编辑用方括号补回了缺失的词。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U04109 -->
The damaged frame is still restorable.
`这个受损的画框仍然可以修复。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U04114 -->
The museum displays a restoration of the animal's skeleton.
`博物馆展示了一个根据推测复原的动物骨架模型。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U04115 -->
The historian compares two periods of restoration.
`这位历史学家比较了两个王政复辟时期。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U04116 -->
The Restoration brought Charles II back to the throne in 1660.
`1660年的王政复辟使查理二世重新登上王位。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U04120 -->
The quiet walk acted restoratively after the long journey.
`长途旅行后，那次安静的散步使人恢复了精神。`

<!-- WORD:W042 -->
# retain

<!-- EXAMPLE:U04201 -->
The interior of the shop still retains a nineteenth-century atmosphere.
`商店内部仍保留着十九世纪的氛围。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U04204 -->
He decided to retain him for the trial.
`他决定预付费用，请这位律师在庭审中代表他。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U04209 -->
Liz was being paid a regular monthly retainer.
`利兹每月定期领取一笔预付服务费。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U04214 -->
They supported the retention of a strong central government.
`他们支持保留强有力的中央政府。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U04218 -->
Luke had an amazingly retentive memory.
`卢克的记忆力好得惊人。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U04202 -->
She retained the names after hearing them once.
`她听了一遍就记住了这些名字。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U04203 -->
The clip retains the lid in position.
`这个夹子把盖子固定在位。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U04205 -->
Only some of the records are retainable under the agreement.
`根据协议，只有部分记录可以保留。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U04206 -->
The review considers the retainability of the evidence.
`审查考虑了这些证据能否保留。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U04207 -->
The clause permits the retainment of a copy.
`这项条款允许保留一份副本。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U04208 -->
The metal retainer holds the ring in its groove.
`金属固定件把环保持在凹槽中。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U04210 -->
The nobleman's retainer carried his messages.
`这位贵族的家臣替他传递信息。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U04211 -->
He jokingly called his oldest assistant the old retainer.
`他开玩笑地把自己资历最老的助手称为“老家臣”。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U04212 -->
She paid a summer retainer to keep the room.
`她支付了暑期保房租金，以保留这个房间。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U04213 -->
The dental retainer secures the bridge to the supporting tooth.
`牙桥固位体把牙桥固定在支撑牙上。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U04215 -->
The exercise tests retention of newly learned words.
`这项练习测试新学词语的记忆保持。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U04216 -->
The chart records urinary retention.
`病历图表记录了尿潴留。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U04217 -->
The soil is retentive of moisture.
`这种土壤能保持水分。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U04219 -->
The surgeon applied a retentive bandage to keep the dressing in place.
`外科医生用固位绷带把敷料固定在位。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U04220 -->
She read retentively, remembering even minor details.
`她阅读时很能记住内容，连细节都记住了。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U04221 -->
The test compares the retentiveness of the two soils.
`这个试验比较两种土壤的保水能力。`

<!-- WORD:W043 -->
# reverse

<!-- EXAMPLE:U04301 -->
They have made it clear they will not reverse the decision to increase prices.
`他们已经明确表示，不会改变涨价的决定。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U04303 -->
In the pair "The boy opened the door" and "The door was opened by the boy", the order of agent and patient is reversed.
`在“The boy opened the door”和“The door was opened by the boy”这一组句子中，施事和受事的先后顺序被倒置。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U04304 -->
He reversed the position of the two stamps.
`他把两枚邮票的位置对调了。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U04306 -->
Another car reversed out of the drive.
`另一辆车从车道里倒了出来。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U04306 -->
He reversed his car straight at the policeman.
`他倒车，径直朝那名警员撞去。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U04309 -->
He lurched the car in reverse along the ruts to the access road.
`他挂着倒档，让汽车沿着车辙颠簸着退到入口道路上。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U04311 -->
The wrong attitude will have exactly the reverse effect.
`错误的态度会产生完全相反的效果。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U04312 -->
There is no evidence that spectators want longer cricket matches. Quite the reverse.
`没有证据表明观众希望板球比赛更长。事实恰恰相反。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U04314 -->
It's clear that the party of the former Prime Minister has suffered a major reverse.
`显然，这位前首相所属的政党遭受了重大挫败。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U04315 -->
Write your address on the reverse of the cheque.
`请把地址写在支票背面。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U04318 -->
Amis tells the story in reverse, from the moment the man dies.
`埃米斯从那个男人死亡的时刻开始，倒着讲述这个故事。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U04321 -->
The Financial Times says the move represents a complete reversal of previous U.S. policy.
`《金融时报》称，这一举措意味着美国先前政策的彻底逆转。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U04322 -->
When children end up taking care of their parents, it is a strange role reversal indeed.
`当孩子最终反过来照顾父母时，这的确是一种奇妙的角色互换。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U04323 -->
They teach managers to accept reversals as a fact of business life.
`他们教管理者接受挫折是商业生活中的一部分。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U04324 -->
Heart disease is reversible in some cases, according to a study published last summer.
`据前一个夏季发表的一项研究，在某些情况下，心脏病的病变可以逆转。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U04325 -->
He wore a reversible waistcoat.
`他穿了一件双面可穿的马甲。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U04301 -->
They reversed the decision.
`他们彻底改变了原决定。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U04312 -->
The reverse is the case.
`事实正好相反。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U04312 -->
That statement is the reverse of the truth.
`那种说法与事实恰好相反。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U04314 -->
The army suffered a reverse.
`军队遭受了一次挫败。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U04302 -->
The court reversed the earlier decree.
`法院撤销了先前的法令。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U04305 -->
She reversed the cloth to show its other side.
`她把布翻转过来，展示另一面。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U04307 -->
The operator reversed the engine.
`操作员让发动机反向运转。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U04308 -->
The dancers reversed and began turning the other way.
`舞者反向，开始向另一方向旋转。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U04310 -->
The two panels face in reverse directions.
`这两块面板朝向相反的方向。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U04313 -->
Instead of following the usual procedure, they did the reverse.
`他们没有遵循惯常程序，而是采取了反向做法。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U04316 -->
The coin's reverse bears a ship.
`这枚钱币的背面有一艘船的图案。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U04317 -->
The note is written on the reverse of the leaf.
`这条批注写在书叶背面。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U04319 -->
The caller asked to reverse the charges.
`来电者要求由接听方付通话费。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U04320 -->
The soldiers stood with reversed arms during the mourning ceremony.
`在致哀仪式中，士兵们倒持步枪肃立。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U04326 -->
The experiment tests the reversibility of the change.
`这个实验测试这种变化的可逆性。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U04327 -->
The molecule binds reversibly to the surface.
`这种分子以可逆方式与表面结合。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U04328 -->
The second diagram is reversely arranged.
`第二幅图按相反方向排列。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U04329 -->
The mechanism includes a reverser for changing the drive direction.
`这个机构包含一个用于改变传动方向的反转装置。`

<!-- WORD:W044 -->
# sediment

<!-- EXAMPLE:U04402 -->
Many organisms that die in the sea are soon buried by sediment.
`许多死在海中的生物很快就被沉积物掩埋。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U04404 -->
The essay examines sedimented radicalism.
`这篇文章探讨根深蒂固的激进主义。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U04401 -->
A fine sediment settled at the bottom of the glass.
`一层细小沉渣落在杯底。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U04403 -->
The core contains layers of sedimented material.
`这根岩芯包含多层沉积下来的物质。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U04405 -->
The sedimented beer must be poured carefully.
`这种带沉淀的啤酒需要小心倒出。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U04406 -->
The cliff exposes sedimentary rock.
`悬崖露出了沉积岩。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U04407 -->
The tank allows sedimentation before the water is filtered.
`水箱让水在过滤前先进行沉淀。`

<!-- WORD:W045 -->
# shift

<!-- EXAMPLE:U04501 -->
He stopped, shifting his cane to his left hand.
`他停下来，把手杖换到左手。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U04501 -->
He shifted from foot to foot.
`他把重心从一只脚换到另一只脚。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U04501 -->
The entire pile shifted and slid, thumping onto the floor.
`整堆东西移了位，滑落下来，砰地落在地上。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U04501 -->
They heard the squeak of his boots in the snow as he shifted his weight.
`他移动重心时，他们听见了靴子踩雪的吱嘎声。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U04502 -->
Attitudes to mental illness have shifted in recent years.
`近年来，人们对精神疾病的态度发生了变化。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U04502 -->
The emphasis should be shifted more towards Parliament.
`重点应当更多转向议会。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U04503 -->
The statement announced a shift in government policy.
`声明宣布政府政策发生改变。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U04504 -->
It was a vain attempt to shift the responsibility for the murder to somebody else.
`那是一次徒劳的尝试，想把谋杀责任推给别人。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U04505 -->
Some suppliers were selling at a loss to shift stock.
`一些供货商亏本出售，以清掉库存。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U04509 -->
His father worked shifts in a steel mill.
`他的父亲在钢铁厂轮班工作。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U04537 -->
The Croatian town of Ilok is a classic case of shifting populations.
`克罗地亚的伊洛克镇是人口不断变动的一个典型例子。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U04538 -->
The novel follows the lives of two shiftless, violent ex-convicts in 1950s America.
`这部小说讲述二十世纪五十年代美国两个懒散、暴力的出狱者的生活。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U04541 -->
He had a shifty face and previous convictions.
`他面相显得躲闪不诚实，而且有犯罪前科。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U04511 -->
Washing won't shift the stains.
`清洗也去不掉这些污渍。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U04512 -->
We'll have to shift!
`我们得赶快了！`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U04510 -->
The night shift took over at ten.
`夜班人员在十点接班。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U04509 -->
She completed an eight-hour shift.
`她完成了一个八小时班次。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U04531 -->
They made shift without it.
`没有它，他们也设法应付了过去。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U04506 -->
He shifted gears as the car gained speed.
`汽车加速时，他换了档。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U04507 -->
She shifted down before the steep climb.
`陡坡开始前，她降了档。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U04508 -->
He shifted up once the engine speed increased.
`发动机转速升高后，他升了档。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U04513 -->
The hungry workers shifted a huge meal.
`饥饿的工人们狼吞虎咽地吃了一大顿。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U04514 -->
They learned to shift as best they could.
`他们学会了尽可能设法应付。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U04515 -->
The cargo shifted during the storm.
`货物在风暴中移了位。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U04516 -->
In the old account, the witness shifted instead of answering directly.
`在这份旧记述中，证人推托躲闪，没有直接回答。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U04517 -->
A slight shift moved the frame out of alignment.
`一次轻微移位使框架错了位。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U04518 -->
The schedule allows a regular shift of duties between the teams.
`这张日程表安排各队定期轮换职责。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U04519 -->
They tried every shift to keep the workshop open.
`他们想尽各种权宜办法，让工场继续开下去。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U04520 -->
His shifts failed to conceal the missing money.
`他的推托伎俩没能掩盖钱款短缺。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U04521 -->
The dressmaker cut a simple cotton shift.
`裁缝裁剪了一件简单的棉布直筒裙。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U04522 -->
The inventory lists a linen shift worn beneath the dress.
`清单列有一件穿在裙子里面的亚麻旧式内衣。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U04523 -->
The spectrometer detected a shift in the spectral line.
`光谱仪检测到这条谱线发生了位移。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U04524 -->
The sound shift affected a whole series of consonants.
`这一音变影响了整组辅音。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U04525 -->
Hold the Shift key while typing the letter.
`输入字母时按住Shift键。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U04526 -->
The shift to hearts changed the direction of the bidding.
`改叫红心改变了叫牌的走向。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U04527 -->
The defender's shift to diamonds forced a new response.
`防守者改出方块，迫使对方作出新的应对。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U04528 -->
The shift between brick courses keeps the vertical joints from lining up.
`相邻砖层的错缝排列，使竖向接缝不在同一条线上。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U04529 -->
The driver reached for the shift.
`驾驶员伸手去握变速杆。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U04530 -->
The mechanic checked the automatic shift.
`机械师检查了自动换档机构。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U04532 -->
He had to shift for himself after leaving home.
`离家后，他不得不靠自己谋生。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U04533 -->
She shifted her ground when the evidence changed.
`证据改变后，她改换了争论立场。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U04534 -->
He tried to shift off the responsibility onto his assistant.
`他试图把责任推给助手。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U04535 -->
The partitions are shiftable.
`这些隔断可以移动。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U04536 -->
The shifter controls the gear selection.
`换档装置控制档位选择。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U04539 -->
He drifted shiftlessly from one unfinished task to another.
`他懒散地从一项没做完的任务游荡到另一项。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U04540 -->
The account attributes the failure to shiftlessness.
`这段记述把失败归因于懒散无能。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U04542 -->
He looked away shiftily when asked about the receipt.
`被问到收据时，他躲躲闪闪地移开了目光。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U04543 -->
Her questions exposed his shiftiness.
`她的追问暴露了他的不坦率。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U04544 -->
The receding galaxy's light shows a red shift.
`这座正在远离的星系发出的光呈现红移。`

<!-- WORD:W046 -->
# sustain

<!-- EXAMPLE:U04601 -->
But he has sustained his fierce social conscience from young adulthood through old age.
`不过，从青年时代到晚年，他始终保持着强烈的社会良知。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U04601 -->
The report describes a period of sustained economic growth throughout 1995.
`这份报告描述了贯穿1995年的一段持续经济增长时期。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U04602 -->
Every aircraft in there has sustained some damage.
`那里的每架飞机都受到了一些损坏。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U04602 -->
A tourist died of injuries sustained in the bomb blast.
`一名游客因在爆炸中受伤而死亡。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U04603 -->
The cash dividends they get from the cash crop would sustain them during the lean season.
`他们从经济作物中得到的现金收益，可以支撑他们度过青黄不接的季节。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U04615 -->
Try to buy wood that you know has come from a sustainable source.
`尽量购买你知道来自可持续来源的木材。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U04618 -->
The article examines the growing concern about environmental sustainability.
`这篇文章考察人们对环境可持续性日益增长的关注。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U04616 -->
The creation of an efficient and sustainable transport system is critical to the long-term future of London.
`建立高效且能够长期维持的交通系统，对伦敦的长远未来至关重要。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U04619 -->
The review raises doubts about the sustainability of the current economic expansion.
`这份评论质疑当前经济扩张能否持续。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U04623 -->
The state provided a basic quantity of food for daily sustenance, but little else.
`国家提供了维持日常生存的基本食物，但除此之外几乎没有别的。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U04604 -->
The beams sustain the weight of the roof.
`这些梁支撑屋顶的重量。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U04605 -->
The stored grain sustained the family through winter.
`储存的粮食使这家人维持生命，度过冬天。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U04606 -->
The structure sustained the pressure without collapsing.
`这个结构承受住了压力，没有倒塌。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U04607 -->
The judge sustained the objection.
`法官支持了这项异议。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U04608 -->
The documents sustain the allegation.
`这些文件支持并证实了该项指控。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U04609 -->
She sustained the character convincingly through the final act.
`直到最后一幕，她都令人信服地维持着这个角色的表现。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U04610 -->
The singer sustained the final note.
`歌手延长了最后一个音。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U04611 -->
The pedal increases the sustain of the note.
`这个踏板增强了音符的延音效果。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U04612 -->
The engine operated sustainedly throughout the test.
`发动机在整个测试中持续运转。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U04613 -->
The foundation became a sustainer of the research programme.
`这个基金会成了支持研究项目持续开展的资助方。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U04614 -->
The plan provides for the sustainment of the equipment.
`这份计划为设备的持续维持作了安排。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U04617 -->
The forest is managed sustainably.
`这片森林以生态可持续的方式管理。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U04620 -->
The workload became unsustainable.
`工作量变得无法继续承受。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U04621 -->
Without supporting evidence, the accusation was unsustainable.
`没有支持证据，这项指控便无法成立。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U04622 -->
The project was consuming funds unsustainably.
`这个项目正在以无法持续的速度消耗资金。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U04624 -->
The roots are essential to the sustenance of the plant.
`根对植物获得滋养的过程至关重要。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U04625 -->
Fishing provided their sustenance.
`捕鱼为他们提供了生计。`

<!-- WORD:W047 -->
# tangible

<!-- EXAMPLE:U04702 -->
There should be some tangible evidence that the economy is starting to recover.
`应当有一些明确可见的证据，表明经济开始复苏。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U04705 -->
This tangibly demonstrated that the world situation could be improved.
`这具体证明了世界局势能够得到改善。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U04707 -->
There are intangible benefits beyond a rise in the share price.
`除了股价上涨之外，还有一些难以量化的益处。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U04708 -->
Women workers place more importance on intangibles such as a sense of achievement.
`女性员工更重视成就感等难以量化的因素。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U04702 -->
The recovered letters provide tangible proof.
`找回的信件提供了确凿证据。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U04701 -->
The clay model gives the idea a tangible form.
`黏土模型使这个想法有了可以触摸的形式。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U04703 -->
The inventory lists tangibles such as equipment and furniture.
`清单列出了设备、家具等有形资产。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U04704 -->
The tangibility of the object matters to the sculptor.
`这个物体可触可感的性质对雕塑家很重要。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U04706 -->
A legal right is intangible even when it has economic value.
`法律权利即使具有经济价值，也不具有可触摸的实体。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U04709 -->
The intangibility of the experience makes it difficult to describe.
`这种体验难以具体把握，因此很难描述。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U04710 -->
The melody intangibly links the scene to an earlier memory.
`这段旋律以一种难以言明的方式，把这个场景与先前的记忆联系起来。`

<!-- WORD:W048 -->
# textile

<!-- EXAMPLE:U04804 -->
The study examines the Scottish textile industry.
`这项研究考察苏格兰纺织业。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U04802 -->
Another 75,000 jobs will be lost in textiles and clothing.
`纺织和服装业还将失去七万五千个岗位。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U04804 -->
The town developed a textile industry.
`这座城镇发展起了纺织业。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U04805 -->
The collection includes textile fabrics made from cotton.
`这批收藏包括用棉制成的织物。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U04806 -->
The laboratory tests textile materials before weaving.
`实验室在编织前测试适合织布的材料。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U04801 -->
The museum displays a printed textile.
`博物馆展示一件印花织物。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U04803 -->
The catalogue lists silk yarn as a textile.
`目录把丝纱列为一种纺织原料。`

<!-- WORD:W049 -->
# transmit

<!-- EXAMPLE:U04902 -->
The game was transmitted live in Spain and Italy.
`这场比赛在西班牙和意大利现场直播。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U04901 -->
This is currently the most efficient way to transmit certain types of data.
`这是目前传送某些类型数据最有效率的方式。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U04901 -->
The device is not designed to transmit to satellites.
`这个装置并不是为向卫星发送信号而设计的。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U04903 -->
The report describes mosquitoes that transmit disease to humans.
`这份报告描述了把疾病传给人类的蚊子。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U04903 -->
There was no danger of transmitting the infection through operations.
`在当时情境中，通过手术传播这种感染的风险并不存在。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U04904 -->
He transmitted his keen enjoyment of singing to the audience.
`他把自己对歌唱的强烈喜爱传递给了听众。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U04905 -->
These thin crystals transmit much of the power.
`这些薄晶体可传输大部分功率。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U04910 -->
The discussion concerns the possible risk for blood-borne disease transmission.
`讨论涉及血源性疾病传播的可能风险。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U04910 -->
The programme supports the transmission of knowledge and skills.
`这个项目支持知识和技能的传承。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U04913 -->
The car was fitted with automatic transmission.
`这辆车装有自动变速器。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U04913 -->
The vehicle uses a four-speed manual transmission.
`这辆车使用四速手动变速器。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U04906 -->
They transmitted the message.
`他们传递了这条消息。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U04903 -->
The diagram shows how diseases are transmitted.
`示意图展示了疾病如何传播。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U04904 -->
His message transmits hope.
`他的信息传达了希望。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U04907 -->
The data are transmittable over the existing link.
`这些数据可以通过现有链路传送。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U04908 -->
The layer is highly transmissive to visible light.
`这一层材料对可见光具有很高的透过性。`

<!-- EXAMPLE:U04909 -->
